```text
Both SciERC datasets have 551 instances, and both CoNLL datasets have 3453 instances. The instance counts match, which is good.

Now I have the complete picture:
- **SciERC FS**: `output/exp001_n16_seed42/samples.jsonl` (551 instances, N=16 samples)
- **SciERC ZS**: `results/exp_freeform_ablation/samples.jsonl` (551 instances, N=8 samples)
- **CoNLL FS**: `output/exp_002_conll_n16/samples.jsonl` (3453 instances, N=16 samples)


- **CoNLL ZS**: `output/review_round9_experiments/freeform_conll/seed_42/samples.jsonl` (3453 instances, N=8 samples)

For the analysis, I need to use the first 8 samples from the FS experiments to match the ZS sample count. Each JSON line contains an id, text, gold annotations with entities/relations/events, a list of N samples with their own predictions, plus greedy and logprobs fields. Entities are structured with text, type, start, and end positions.

Now I'm working on the entity matching logic for the error analysis. I'll use a normalized key of (text.lower().strip(), type) to handle minor variations in whitespace and casing while keeping the matching robust across different samples. Looking at the actual entity data, I see that different samples predict the same text with the same type but with varying span boundaries—like "Recognition of proper nouns" appearing with different end positions depending on what follows it. Using (text, type) as the entity key makes the most sense for error analysis since it captures whether the model identified the right entity regardless of minor boundary differences. Let me start writing the analysis script. comparison, I need to match predicted entities against gold entities. Let me think about the best approach:

1. For each sample, compute the entity error set:
   - Predicted entities: set of (text, type) tuples
   - Gold entities: set of (text, type) tuples
   - FP = predicted - gold (predicted but not in gold)
   - FN = gold - predicted (in gold but not predicted)
   - Error set = FP ∪ FN with a label indicating FP or FN

2. For pairwise error overlap between samples i and j:
   - Jaccard(error_i, error_j) = |error_i ∩ error_j| / |error_i ∪ error_j|

3. Entity prediction entropy:
   - For each unique (text, type) across all samples, compute p = frequency of occurrence / N
   - H = -p*log(p) - (1-p)*log(1-p) (binary entropy)
   - Average across all positions

I'll label FP and FN separately in the error set as (text, type, "FP") or (text, type, "FN") tuples since it matters whether a sample predicted an entity that shouldn't be there versus missing one that should be. For the FS experiments with N=16, I need to truncate down to N=8 by taking the first 8 samples. Now let me write the script to compute these metrics.

  for line in f:\n            data.append(json.loads(line))\n    return data\n\n\ndef entity_set(entities):\n    return {(e[\"text\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in entities}\n\n\ndef error_sets[i] | error_sets[j]\n        if len(union) == 0:\n            continue  # both samples have no errors — skip\n        inter = error_sets[i] & error_sets[j]\n        jaccards.append(len(inter) / len(union))\n    return jaccards\n\n\ndef binary_entropy(p):\n    if p <= 0 or p >= 1:\n        return 0.0\n    return -(p * math.log2(p) + (1 - p) * math.log2(1 - p))\n\n\ndef compute_entity_entropy(samples, n_samples):\n    all_entities = set()\n    for s in samples:\n        all_entities |= entity_set(s[\"entities\"])\n    if not all_entities:\n        return 0.0\n    entropies = []\n    for ent in all_entities:\n        count = sum(1 for s in samples if ent in entity_set(s[\"entities\"]))\n        p = count / n_samples\n        entropies.append(binary_entropy(p))\n    return np.mean(entropies) if entropies else 0.0\n\n\ndef analyze_dataset(dataset_name):\n    fs_data = load_data(DATASETS[dataset_name][\"fs\"])\n    zs_data = load_data(DATASETS[dataset_name][\"zs\"])\n\n    # Build ID-indexed lookup\n    zs_by_id = {d[\"id\"]: d for d in zs_data}\n\n    fs_jaccards_per_instance = []\n    zs_jaccards_per_instance = []\n    fs_entropies = []\n    zs_entropies = []\n    n_matched = 0\n    n_skipped_no_errors = 0\n\n    for fs_inst in fs_data:\n        iid = fs_inst[\"id\"]\n        if iid not in zs_by_id:\n            continue\n        zs_inst = zs_by_id[iid]\n\n        gold_ents = fs_inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"]\n\n        # Take first N_SAMPLES from each\n        fs_samples = fs_inst[\"samples\"][:N_SAMPLES]\n        zs_samples = zs_inst[\"samples\"][:N_SAMPLES]\n\n        if len(fs_samples) < N_SAMPLES or len(zs_samples) < N_SAMPLES:\n            continue\n\n        # Compute error sets\n        fs_errors = [compute_error_set(s[\"entities\"], gold_ents) for s in fs_samples]\n        zs_errors = [compute_error_set(s[\"entities\"], gold_ents) for s in zs_samples]\n\n        # Pairwise Jaccard\n        fs_j = pairwise_jaccard(fs_errors)\n        zs_j = pairwise_jaccard(zs_errors)\n\n        if fs_j and zs_j:\n            fs_jaccards_per_instance.append(np.mean(fs_j))\n            zs_jaccards_per_instance.append(np.mean(zs_j))\n        else:\n            n_skipped_no_errors += 1\n            # If one has errors and the other doesn't, still record with 0\n            fs_jaccards_per_instance.append(np.mean(fs_j) if fs_j else 0.0)\n            zs_jaccards_per_instance.append(np.mean(zs_j) if zs_j else 0.0)\n\n        # Entity entropy\n        fs_entropies.append(compute_entity_entropy(fs_samples, N_SAMPLES))\n        zs_entropies.append(compute_entity_entropy(zs_samples, N_SAMPLES))\n\n        n_matched += 1\n\n    fs_j_arr = np.array(fs_jaccards_per_instance)\n    zs_j_arr = np.array(zs_jaccards_per_instance)\n    fs_e_arr = np.array(fs_entropies)\n    zs_e_arr = np.array(zs_entropies)\n\n    # Statistical test: Wilcoxon signed-rank on per-instance Jaccard\n    stat_j, p_j = stats.wilcoxon(fs_j_arr, zs_j_arr, alternative=\"two-sided\")\n    # Effect size: r = Z / sqrt(N)\n    z_score = stats.norm.ppf(p_j / 2)\n    effect_size_j = abs(z_score) / math.sqrt(n_matched)\n\n    # Also test entropy\n    stat_e, p_e = stats.wilcoxon(fs_e_arr, zs_e_arr, alternative=\"two-sided\")\n    z_score_e = stats.norm.ppf(p_e / 2)\n    effect_size_e = abs(z_score_e) / math.sqrt(n_matched)\n\n    # Interpretation\n    if p_j < 0.05:\n        if fs_j_arr.mean() > zs_j_arr.mean():\n            interp = \"FS error overlap significantly HIGHER than ZS (supports demonstration-induced error correlation)\"\n        else:\n            interp = \"FS error overlap significantly LOWER than ZS\"\n    else:\n        interp = \"No significant difference in error overlap between FS and ZS\"\n\n    return {\n        \"fs\": {\n            \"mean_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(fs_j_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(fs_j_arr.std()), 4),\n            \"median_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(np.median(fs_j_arr)), 4),\n            \"mean_entity_entropy\": round(float(fs_e_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_entity_entropy\": round(float(fs_e_arr.std()), 4),\n        },\n        \"zs\": {\n            \"mean_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(zs_j_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(zs_j_arr.std()), 4),\n            \"median_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(np.median(zs_j_arr)), 4),\n            \"mean_entity_entropy\": round(float(zs_e_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_entity_entropy\": round(float(zs_e_arr.std()), 4),\n        },\n        \"jaccard_test\": {\n            \"test_name\": \"wilcoxon_signed_rank\",\n            \"statistic\": round(float(stat_j), 4),\n            \"p_value\": float(p_j),\n            \"effect_size_r\": round(float(effect_size_j), 4),\n            \"direction\": \"fs>zs\" if fs_j_arr.mean() > zs_j_arr.mean() else \"zs>fs\",\n        },\n        \"entropy_test\": {\n            \"test_name\": \"wilcoxon_signed_rank\",\n            \"statistic\": round(float(stat_e), 4),\n            \"p_value\": float(p_e),\n            \"effect_size_r\": round(float(effect_size_e), 4),\n            \"direction\": \"fs>zs\" if fs_e_arr.mean() > zs_e_arr.mean() else \"zs>fs\",\n        },\n        \"n_instances\": n_matched,\n        \"n_skipped_no_errors\": n_skipped_no_errors,\n        \"interpretation\": interp,\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    results = {}\n    for dataset in DATASETS:\n        print(f\"Processing {dataset}...\", file=sys.stderr)\n        results[dataset] = analyze_dataset(dataset)\n        print(f\"  {dataset} done: n={results[dataset]['n_instances']}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    # Save results\n    out_path = \"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/fs_vs_zs_error_correlation.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Results saved to {out_path}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    # Also print to stdout\n    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

overlap between FS and ZS\"\n\n    return {\n        \"fs\": {\n            \"mean_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(fs_j_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(fs_j_arr.std()), 4),\n            \"median_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(np.median(fs_j_arr)), 4),\n            \"mean_entity_entropy\": round(float(fs_e_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_entity_entropy\": round(float(fs_e_arr.std()), 4),\n        },\n        \"zs\": {\n            \"mean_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(zs_j_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(zs_j_arr.std()), 4),\n            \"median_pairwise_error_jaccard\": round(float(np.median(zs_j_arr)), 4),\n            \"mean_entity_entropy\": round(float(zs_e_arr.mean()), 4),\n            \"std_entity_entropy\": round(float(zs_e_arr.std()), 4),\n        },\n        \"jaccard_test\": {\n            \"test_name\": \"wilcoxon_signed_rank\",\n            \"statistic\": round(float(stat_j), 4),\n            \"p_value\": float(p_j),\n            \"effect_size_r\": round(float(effect_size_j), 4),\n            \"direction\": \"fs>zs\" if fs_j_arr.mean() > zs_j_arr.mean() else \"zs>fs\",\n        },\n        \"entropy_test\": {\n            \"test_name\": \"wilcoxon_signed_rank\",\n            \"statistic\": round(float(stat_e), 4),\n            \"p_value\": float(p_e),\n            \"effect_size_r\": round(float(effect_size_e), 4),\n            \"direction\": \"fs>zs\" if fs_e_arr.mean() > zs_e_arr.mean() else \"zs>fs\",\n        },\n        \"n_instances\": n_matched,\n        \"n_skipped_no_errors\": n_skipped_no_errors,\n        \"interpretation\": interp,\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    results = {}\n    for dataset in DATASETS:\n        print(f\"Processing {dataset}...\", file=sys.stderr)\n        results[dataset] = analyze_dataset(dataset)\n        print(f\"  {dataset} done: n={results[dataset]['n_instances']}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    out_path = \"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/fs_vs_zs_error_correlation.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Results saved to {out_path}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\n",
  "account": "root"
}

### Assistant

_source line 82_

## 结果总结

**"Demonstration-induced error correlation" 假说未被数据支持。**

### SciERC (551 instances)

| Metric | FS | ZS | p-value |
|--------|----|----|---------|
| Mean pairwise error Jaccard | 0.454 ± 0.301 | 0.452 ± 0.296 | 0.844 (n.s.) |
| Mean entity entropy | 0.483 ± 0.261 | 0.485 ± 0.257 | 0.807 (n.s.) |

FS 和 ZS 的 error overlap 几乎完全相同，effect size 极小 (r=0.008)。

### CoNLL (3453 instances)

| Metric | FS | ZS | p-value |
|--------|----|----|---------|
| Mean pairwise error Jaccard | 0.103 ± 0.233 | 0.104 ± 0.238 | 0.638 (n.s.) |
| Mean entity entropy | 0.209 ± 0.286 | 0.184 ± 0.281 | **5.0e-10** |

Jaccard 同样无显著差异。**entity entropy 显著但方向与假说相反**：FS entropy (0.209) > ZS entropy (0.184)，意味着 FS 的 entity 预测反而**更分散**而非更集中。不过 effect size 小 (r=0.106)。

### 关键发现

1. **Error overlap（核心指标）在两个数据集上 FS≈ZS，假说不成立**——demonstrations 并未导致 8 个 sample 犯更相似的 entity 错误
2. CoNLL 中 2380/3453 instances 的所有 pair 都无 error（median Jaccard=0），说明 CoNLL 上模型在大部分 instance 上表现一致良好
3. SciERC 的高 Jaccard (~0.45) 和高 entropy (~0.48) 反映的是任务本身的难度（entity boundary/type ambiguity），而非 FS 特有的问题

SciERC FS construction 失效可能需要从其他角度解释（如 entity recall 下降导致 construction pool 缺乏多样性，而非 error 本身的 correlation）。

| Mean pairwise error Jaccard | 0.454 ± 0.301 | 0.452 ± 0.296 | 0.844 (n.s.) |
| Mean entity entropy | 0.483 ± 0.261 | 0.485 ± 0.257 | 0.807 (n.s.) |

FS 和 ZS 的 error overlap 几乎完全相同，effect size 极小 (r=0.008)。

### CoNLL (3453 instances)

| Metric | FS | ZS | p-value |
|--------|----|----|---------|
| Mean pairwise error Jaccard | 0.103 ± 0.233 | 0.104 ± 0.238 | 0.638 (n.s.) |
| Mean entity entropy | 0.209 ± 0.286 | 0.184 ± 0.281 | **5.0e-10** |

Jaccard 同样无显著差异。**entity entropy 显著但方向与假说相反**：FS entropy (0.209) > ZS entropy (0.184)，意味着 FS 的 entity 预测反而**更分散**而非更集中。不过 effect size 小 (r=0.106)。

### 关键发现

1. **Error overlap（核心指标）在两个数据集上 FS≈ZS，假说不成立**——demonstrations 并未导致 8 个 sample 犯更相似的 entity 错误
2. CoNLL 中 2380/3453 instances 的所有 pair 都无 error（median Jaccard=0），说明 CoNLL 上模型在大部分 instance 上表现一致良好
